```
# Task 13a – Search backend: IGDB aliases, typo-tolerant `q`, suggestions endpoint

**Model:** Opus · **Effort:** high
Runs **in parallel with Task 13b** (search UI), which works in a separate worktree against the API contract below. Merge 13a first.

## Goal
Search is one of the two "deep" parts. Visitors must find games by partial names, with typos and with common abbreviations ("gta", "rdr2").

## Decisions (made by the owner – do not change)
- Q1. **Aliases from IGDB `alternative_names`.** Fetch them for the **existing 50 games only** – the catalog itself must not change (no new games, no changed popularity/covers/summaries).
- Q3. When `q` is set, the default sort is **`relevance`** ("Best match": match quality, ties by popularity). Filters and facet counts work together with `q`.
- Q4. Search scope: game **title + aliases** only (not developer/publisher/genre).
- `q`: trimmed; length **2–100** characters, otherwise `400` with a clear message. Never string-built SQL.

## 1. Branch
Standard workflow from `CLAUDE.md` (`task/13a-search-backend`).

## 2. Aliases data
- `cmd/igdb-snapshot`: add a mode (e.g. `--aliases-only`) that reads the committed `seed/igdb_snapshot.json`, queries IGDB `alternative_names` for those 50 `igdb_id`s, and writes them into the snapshot **without changing any other field** (verify with a diff: only the new alias field changes). You run it locally – the IGDB credentials are in the owner's `.env` and never leave it.
- Keep only useful aliases: Lati
```

```
// Reusable API client: every API call in the app goes through apiFetch.

/** How long a request may run before we give up (ms). */
export const REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS = 15_000;

export type ApiErrorCode = "http" | "network" | "timeout" | "parse";

/**
 * The only error type apiFetch throws (besides a caller-initiated abort).
 * - http:    server answered with non-2xx; `status` and parsed `body` are set
 * - network: request never got an answer (server down, offline)
 * - timeout: no answer within the timeout
 * - parse:   2xx answer that is not valid JSON
 */
export class ApiError extends Error {
  readonly code: ApiErrorCode;
  readonly status: number; // 0 when there was no HTTP response
  readonly body: unknown; // parsed JSON error body, if the server sent one

  constructor(code: ApiErrorCode, message: string, status = 0, body?: unknown) {
    super(message);
    this.name = "ApiError";
    this.code = code;
    this.status = status;
    this.body = body;
  }
}

export type ApiOptions = Omit<RequestInit, "signal"> & {
  /** Abort from outside (e.g. React effect cleanup). */
  signal?: AbortSignal;
  /** Override the default timeout. */
  timeoutMs?: number;
};

/**
 * fetch + JSON parsing + typed errors + timeout.
 * Resolves with the parsed JSON body of a 2xx response, otherwise throws ApiError.
 * If the caller's own `signal` aborts, the original AbortError is rethrown.
 */
export async function apiFetch<T>(path: string, options: ApiOptions = {}): Promise<T> {
```

```
# Task 13b – Search UI: header suggestions, search results on the Store page

**Model:** Opus · **Effort:** high
**Runs in parallel with Task 13a** (backend). Another session works in the main folder, so use a separate worktree:
ʼʼʼ
git worktree add ../ENEBA-homework-13b -b task/13b-search-ui origin/main
ʼʼʼ
Build against the API contract in `docs/tasks/TASK_13a_search_backend.md` §4 (copy of it below). Until 13a is merged, use mocked `apiFetch` in tests and a small local mock for the browser check. After 13a is merged, rebase on `main` and verify against the real API before asking for review. Remove the worktree after the PR is opened.

## API contract (from 13a)
- `GET /api/products?q=…` – `q` (2–100 chars) filters items and facets; sort `relevance` ("Best match") is the default when `q` is set.
- `GET /api/search/suggest?q=…` → `{ items: [{ gameTitle, productSlug, platform{slug,name}, region{slug,name}, coverUrl, priceCents|null, inStock, matchedAlias|null }] }`, max 6, one per game, relevance order. Types and `getSuggestions(q)` are added by 13a in `src/api/` – if they are not on `main` yet, add identical ones and resolve on rebase.

## Decisions (made by the owner – do not change)
- Q2. **Header suggestions** (desktop field and the phone search overlay), ARIA 1.2 **combobox + listbox** pattern:
  - from 2 characters, debounced ~200 ms, TanStack Query with `keepPreviousData` (no flicker while typing), cancel/ignore stale responses;
  - up to 6 rows: small cover, title (matched part highlighted when it is a plain substring), "matched alias" hint when present (e.g. *GTA V*), platform · region, price or "Out of stock";
  - last row: "See all results for 'q'" → `/store?q=…`;
  - keyboard: ↓/↑ move (wraps), Enter on a row → product page, Enter with no active row → `/store?q=…`, Esc closes (second Esc clears), Tab leaves; mouse click on a row works; click outside closes;
  - loading: subtle spinner in the field; no results: "No matches for 'q'" row + the "See all results" row is hidden.
  - Hover/focus prefetch of the product query, like Store cards.
- Q3/Q5. **Store page with `q`:**
  - remove the Task 11 note "Text search arrives in a later task…"; send `q` to the API;
  - heading "Results for 'q'" (+ result count as now) and a "Clear search" control that removes only `q`;
  - sort select shows "Best match" **only when `q` is set** (default then); if `q` is removed while sort is `re
```

```
// Usage: node ref13.mjs <desktop|mobile> <query> [<query> ...]
import { launch, open, hidePopups, SCRATCH } from "./lib.mjs";

await hidePopups(page);
const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));

async function findInput() {
  return page.evaluateHandle(() => {
    const inputs = [...document.querySelectorAll("input")].filter((i) => {
      const r = i.getBoundingClientRect();
      return r.width > 0 && r.height > 0 && (i.type === "search" || i.type === "text");
    });
    return inputs[0] ?? null;
  });
}

let input = await findInput();
```

```
const input = await page.$("input[role=combobox]");
if (!input) { console.log("no combobox input visible"); }
else {
  await input.click();
  await page.keyboard.type(q, { delay: 80 });
  await sleep(3000);
  await page.screenshot({ path: `${SCRATCH}/ref/search-${q.replace(/\W+/g, "_")}-${kind}.png` });
  await inspect(page, [
    { label: "row title", sel: "[role=listbox] a[role=option] div div div", index: 1, up: 0 },
    { label: "all row descendants (first row)", sel: "[role=listbox] a[role=option]", index: 0, up: 0 },
    { label: "show all", sel: "[role=listbox] li:last-child button", up: 1 },
    { label: "first option button svg", sel: "[role=listbox] button svg", up: 0 },
  ]);
  const deep = await page.evaluate(() => {
    const a = document.querySelector("[role=listbox] a[role=option]");
    if (!a) return document.querySelector(".ReactModal__Content")?.innerText ?? "(no popup)";
    return [...a.querySelectorAll("*")].map((e) => {
      const cs = getComputedStyle(e); const r = e.getBoundingClientRect();
      const own = [...e.childNodes].filter((n) => n.nodeType === 3).map((n) => n.textContent).join("").trim();
      return `${e.tagName} x${Math.round(r.x)} y${Math.round(r.y)} w${Math.round(r.width)} h${Math.round(r.height)} fs=${cs.fontSize} fw=${cs.fontWeight} lh=${cs.lineHeight} c=${cs.color} td=${cs.textDecorationLine} "${own.slice(0, 40)}"`;
    }).join("\n");
  });
  console.log(deep);
}
await browser.close();
```

```
// One QueryClient for the whole app = one cache. TanStack Query keeps every server response in it,
// so a page that was already visited can show its data immediately (no empty flash).
// All requests inside it still go through apiFetch (src/api/client.ts), never through a raw fetch.
import { QueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { ApiError } from "../api/client.ts";

/**
 * Retry rule: ask again ONCE, and only when asking again can help.
 * - network error / 5xx: the server may just be waking up (Neon, cold start) -> retry once.
 * - 4xx (404, 400...): the answer would be the same -> do not retry.
 * - timeout: the user already waited 15 s; retrying would double that -> do not retry.
 * - anything else (parse error, a bug in our code): retrying does not help.
 * `failureCount` is 0 after the first failure.
 */
export function shouldRetry(failureCount: number, error: unknown): boolean {
  if (failureCount >= 1) return false;
  if (!(error instanceof ApiError)) return false;
  if (error.code === "network") return true;
  return error.code === "http" && error.status >= 500;
}

export function createQueryClient(): QueryClient {
  return new QueryClient({
    defaultOptions: {
      queries: {
        // staleTime: for 30 s a cached answer counts as fresh. Going back to the Store (or a product)
        // shows the cached data instantly and does NOT refetch. After 30 s it is still shown at once,
        // and silently refreshed in the background. Game data and pric
… [truncated; original 6121 chars]
```

```
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { buildStoreSearch, mergeStoreParams, parseStoreParams } from "./storeParams.ts";
import { buildProductsQuery } from "../api/catalog.ts";

const parse = (search: string) => parseStoreParams(new URLSearchParams(search));

describe("parseStoreParams", () => {
  it("returns an empty object for an empty URL", () => {
    expect(parse("")).toEqual({});
  });

  it("reads every known parameter", () => {
    expect(parse("q=zelda&platform=steam,psn&region=global&minPrice=500&maxPrice=2000&sort=price_asc&page=2")).toEqual({
      q: "zelda",
      platform: ["steam", "psn"],
      region: ["global"],
      minPrice: 500,
      maxPrice: 2000,
      sort: "price_asc",
      page: 2,
    });
  });

  it("keeps minPrice=0 (a real value)", () => {
    expect(parse("minPrice=0")).toEqual({ minPrice: 0 });
  });

  it("drops invalid values instead of passing them on", () => {
    expect(parse("sort=cheapest&page=0&minPrice=abc&maxPrice=-5&page=1.5")).toEqual({});
  });

  it("ignores empty list items and unknown parameters", () => {
    expect(parse("platform=,steam,,&foo=bar")).toEqual({ platform: ["steam"] });
  });

  it("trims the search text and drops it when blank", () => {
    expect(parse("q=%20zelda%20")).toEqual({ q: "zelda" });
    expect(parse("q=%20%20")).toEqual({});
  });
});

describe("buildStoreSearch", () => {
  it("returns an empty string when nothing is set", () => {
    expect(buildStoreSearch({})).toBe("");
  });
```

```
---
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

/**
 * Returns `value`, but only after it has stopped changing for `delayMs`.
 * Typing "witcher" quickly gives ONE change ("witcher") instead of seven ("w", "wi", "wit"…), so the search
 * suggestions send one request instead of one per key. Every new value restarts the timer (the cleanup clears
 * the old one).
 */
export function useDebouncedValue<T>(value: T, delayMs: number): T {
  const [debounced, setDebounced] = useState(value);

  useEffect(() => {
    const timer = setTimeout(() => setDebounced(value), delayMs);
    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [value, delayMs]);

  return debounced;
}
```

**Tool: Write**

```
c:\PROJEKTAI\ENEBA\ENEBA-homework-13b\src\features\search\highlight.ts
---
/** A piece of a title: `match` = this piece is the typed text and is drawn highlighted. */
export interface HighlightPart {
  text: string;
  match: boolean;
}

/**
 * Splits `text` around the FIRST place where `query` appears, ignoring upper/lower case:
 *   highlightParts("The Witcher 3", "witch") -> [{"The ", false}, {"Witch", true}, {"er 3", false}]
 * The highlighted piece keeps the title's own spelling ("Witch", not "witch").
 * No match (e.g. a typo "witchr" or an alias hit "gta") -> the whole text as one plain part: the server found it
 * by similarity or by an alias, so there is no exact piece to mark.
 */
export function highlightParts(text: string, query: string): HighlightPart[] {
  const needle = query.trim().toLowerCase();
  const haystack = text.toLowerCase();
  // A few letters change length when lower-cased (e.g. "İ"); then the positions would not line up. Rare: just skip.
  const index = needle && haystack.length === text.length ? haystack.indexOf(needle) : -1;
  if (index === -1) return [{ text, match: false }];

  const end = index + needle.length;
  const parts: HighlightPart[] = [
    { text: text.slice(0, index), match: false },
    { text: text.slice(index, end), match: true },
    { text: text.slice(end), match: false },
  ];
  return parts.filter((part) => part.text !== "");
}
```

**Tool: Write**

```
c:\PROJEKTAI\ENEBA\ENEBA-homework-13b\src\features\search\moveActive.ts
---
/**
 * Which row of the suggestion list is active after ↓ or ↑. -1 = no active row (the text cursor is "in the field").
 * The list wraps: ↓ on the last row goes to the first, ↑ on the first goes to the last.
 * With nothing active, ↓ starts at the first row and ↑ at the last. An empty list has no active row.
 */
export function moveActive(current: number, count: number, direction: "down" | "up"): number {
  if (count === 0) return -1;
  if (direction === "down") return current < 0 || current >= count - 1 ? 0 : current + 1;
  return current <= 0 ? count - 1 : current - 1;
}
```

```
import type { MouseEvent } from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router";
import type { Suggestion } from "../../api/types.ts";

import { cx } from "../../lib/cx.ts";

interface SuggestionOptionProps {
  item: Suggestion;
  /** DOM id: the input points at the active row with aria-activedescendant. */
  id: string;
  /** The text typed in the field (for the highlight). */
  query: string;
  /** Moved to with ↓/↑: drawn like a hovered row and announced by screen readers. */
  active: boolean;
  onChoose: () => void;
  onPrefetch: () => void;
}

/** Keeps the focus in the text field when a row is clicked (the field must stay the focused element). */
function keepFocusInField(event: MouseEvent) {
  event.preventDefault();
}

/** The title (or alias) with the typed text drawn highlighted. */
function Highlighted({ text, query }: { text: string; query: string }) {
  return highlightParts(text, query).map((part, i) =>
    part.match ? (
      <mark key={i} className={styles.mark}>
        {part.text}
      </mark>
    ) : (
      part.text
    ),
  );
}

      </span>
```

```
/*
 * One row of the header search list: see docs/DESIGN.md 4.13 (measured on eneba.com in Task 13b).
 * Eneba: 72px cover, 15px to the text, 20px side padding, 5px above and below; title 14px bold; price on the right.

.option {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: var(--suggestion-cover-width) minmax(0, 1fr) auto;
  align-items: center;
  gap: var(--space-3);
  padding: var(--space-1) var(--space-4);
  color: var(--color-text);
  transition: background-color var(--transition-fast);
}

/* Hover (mouse) and active (↓/↑) look the same, like Eneba (#351183). */
.option:hover,
.active {
  background: var(--color-bg-deep);
  color: var(--color-text);
}

.cover {
  align-self: start;
}

.body {

  flex-direction: column;
  gap: var(--space-1);

}

.title {

  font-weight: var(--font-weight-bold);
  line-height: var(--line-height-tight);
  overflow-wrap: anywhere;
}

/* The typed text inside the title: yellow instead of the browser's yellow box. */
```

```
 * - Enter on a row opens that product; Enter with no active row (or "See all results") goes to /store?q=….
```

```
---
/*
 * Header search field + suggestions list: see docs/DESIGN.md 3.1, 4.2 and 4.13 (list measured on eneba.com in Task 13b).
 * Desktop: the list hangs under the field, exactly as wide (550px), and the page behind is dimmed.
 * ≤ 930px: the field covers the header row (Header.module.css) and the list spans the whole screen width under it.
 */
.box {
  position: relative; /* the list is placed under the field */
  flex: 1;
  min-width: 0;
}

/* While the list is open, the field and the list sit above the dimmed page (and above the cards' cover badges). */
.boxOpen {
  z-index: var(--z-dropdown);
}

/* ---- the field (moved from SearchField in Task 13b) ---- */
.field {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  width: 100%;
  height: var(--control-height);
  border: 1px solid var(--color-border-strong);
  border-radius: var(--radius-none);
  background: var(--color-bg); /* not transparent: the field stays bright above the dimmed page */
}

@media (min-width: 931px) {
  .field {
    height: var(--search-height);
  }
}

/* The ring goes around the whole field, not around the inner input. */
.field:focus-within {
  outline: var(--focus-ring-width) solid var(--focus-ring-color);
  outline-offset: var(--focus-ring-offset);
}

.submit {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  flex: none;
  min-width: var(--hit-target-min);
  height: 100%;
  padding: 0;
  border: 0;
  background: transparent;
  color: var(--color-text);
  transition: color var(--transition-fast);
}

.submit:hover {
  color: var(--color-accent);
}

.icon {
  width: var(--icon-size-sm);
  height: var(--icon-size-sm);
}

.input {
  flex: 1;
  min-width: 0;
  height: 100%;
  padding: 0 var(--space-2) 0 0;
  border: 0;
  outline: none; /* the wrapper draws the focus ring (:focus-within above) */
  background: transparent;
  color: var(--color-text);
  font-size: var(--font-size-base);
  font-weight: var(--font-weight-bold);
}

@media (min-width: 931px) {
  .input {
    font-size: var(--font-size-lg);
  }
}

.input::placeholder {
  color: var(--color-text-subtle);
  opacity: 1; /* Firefox dims placeholders by default */
}

/* The small spinner at the right end of the field while suggestions load. */
.loading {
  display: flex;
  flex: none;
  padding-right: var(--space-2);
  color: var(--color-text-muted);
}
```

```
import type { ComponentProps, MouseEvent } from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router";
import { cx } from "../lib/cx.ts";
import styles from "./Button.module.css";

export interface ButtonProps extends ComponentProps<"button"> {
  /** primary = the main action (once per view area), secondary = outlined, ghost = text-like. */
  variant?: "primary" | "secondary" | "ghost";
  /** Shows a spinner instead of the label, keeps the size and ignores clicks. */
  loading?: boolean;
}

/**
 * The one button of the app (DESIGN.md 4.1). `disabled` is the normal HTML attribute.
 * While `loading` the button is NOT disabled (it would lose keyboard focus); it is marked
 * aria-busy and its clicks are swallowed instead.
 */
export function Button({
  variant = "primary",
  loading = false,
  type = "button", // a <button> inside a <form> submits by default; make that opt-in
  className,
  children,
  onClick,
  ...rest
}: ButtonProps) {
  function handleClick(event: MouseEvent<HTMLButtonElement>) {
    if (loading) {
      event.preventDefault(); // also stops a submit button from submitting
      return;
    }
    onClick?.(event);
  }

  return (
    <button
      {...rest}
      type={type}
      className={cx(styles.button, styles[variant], loading && styles.loading, className)}
      aria-busy={loading || undefined}
      onClick={handleClick}
    >
      <span className={styles.label}>{children}</span>
      {loading && <span className={styles.spinner} aria-hidden="true" />}
    
… [truncated; original 8036 chars]
```

```
---
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useId } from "react";
import { productListQuery } from "../../api/queries.ts";
import { ProductGrid, ProductGridSkeleton } from "./ProductGrid.tsx";
import styles from "./PopularProducts.module.css";

/** How many products the "Popular right now" block shows (owner decision Q5). */
export const POPULAR_COUNT = 8;

/**
 * "Popular right now": the first 8 products of the default listing (most popular first), shown under
 * "No results for 'q'" so a search that found nothing still offers somewhere to go (owner decision Q5).
 * It is an extra: if it fails to load it simply is not shown (the empty-state message above already explains the page).
 */
export function PopularProducts() {
  const headingId = useId();
  const query = useQuery(productListQuery({ pageSize: POPULAR_COUNT }));

  if (query.isError) return null;

  return (
    <section className={styles.section} aria-labelledby={headingId}>
      <h2 id={headingId} className={styles.title}>
        Popular right now
      </h2>
      {query.data ? <ProductGrid items={query.data.items} /> : <ProductGridSkeleton count={POPULAR_COUNT} />}
    </section>
  );
}
```

```
// @vitest-environment jsdom
// The header search combobox in an in-memory router, with apiFetch replaced by a fake (no server, no network).
// Fake timers drive the 200 ms debounce, so "typing fast" is exact and the tests do not wait for real time.

import { act } from "react";
import { createMemoryRouter, RouterProvider, useLocation, useSearchParams } from "react-router";
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { apiFetch } from "../../api/client.ts";
import type { Suggestion, SuggestionsResponse } from "../../api/types.ts";

  ...(await importOriginal<typeof import("../../api/client.ts")>()),
  apiFetch: vi.fn(),
}));

const fakeApi = vi.mocked(apiFetch);

function makeSuggestion(overrides: Partial<Suggestion> = {}): Suggestion {
  return {
    gameTitle: "The Witcher 3: Wild Hunt",
    productSlug: "the-witcher-3-wild-hunt-steam-global",
    platform: { slug: "steam", name: "Steam" },
    region: { slug: "global", name: "Global" },
    coverUrl: "https://images.example/witcher.jpg",
    priceCents: 1099,
    inStock: true,
    matchedAlias: null,
    ...overrides,
  };
}

  priceCents: null,

});
const gta = makeSuggestion({
  gameTitle: "Grand Theft Auto V",
  productSlug: "gta-v-steam-global",
  matchedAlias: "GTA V",
  priceCents: 1499,
});

/** The suggest requests made so far (the q of each). */
const suggestCalls = () =>
  fakeApi.mock.calls
    .map((call) => call[0])
    .filter((path) => path.startsWith("/api/search/suggest"))
    .map((path) => new URL(path, "http://x").searchParams.get("q"));

/** Answers by search text: "gta" -> GTA, "qwertyzz" -> nothing, anything else -> the two Witcher games. */
function answerFor(path: string): SuggestionsResponse | object {
  if (!path.startsWith("/api/search/suggest")) return {}; // product prefetch
```

```
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { highlightParts } from "./highlight.ts";

describe("highlightParts", () => {
  it("marks the typed part, keeping the title's own spelling", () => {
    expect(highlightParts("The Witcher 3", "witch")).toEqual([
      { text: "The ", match: false },
      { text: "Witch", match: true },
      { text: "er 3", match: false },
    ]);
  });

  it("ignores upper / lower case in both directions", () => {
    expect(highlightParts("portal 2", "PORTAL")).toEqual([
      { text: "portal", match: true },
      { text: " 2", match: false },
    ]);
  });

  it("marks only the first place it appears", () => {
    expect(highlightParts("Dark Souls Dark", "dark")).toEqual([
      { text: "Dark", match: true },
      { text: " Souls Dark", match: false },
    ]);
  });

  it("marks a match at the end (no empty plain part)", () => {
    expect(highlightParts("Elden Ring", "ring")).toEqual([
      { text: "Elden ", match: false },
      { text: "Ring", match: true },
    ]);
  });

  it("ignores spaces around the typed text", () => {
    expect(highlightParts("Hades", "  hades ")).toEqual([{ text: "Hades", match: true }]);
  });

  it("no plain substring (a typo or an alias hit) -> the whole text, not marked", () => {
    expect(highlightParts("The Witcher 3", "witchr")).toEqual([{ text: "The Witcher 3", match: false }]);
    expect(highlightParts("Grand Theft Auto V", "gta")).toEqual([{ text: "Grand Theft Auto V", match: false }]);
  });

  it("an empty text marks nothing", () => {
    expect(highlightParts("Hades", "")).toEqual([{ text: "Hades", match: false }]);
    expect(highlightParts("Hades", "   ")).toEqual([{ text: "Hades", match: false }]);
  });

  it("skips titles whose length changes when lower-cased (the positions would not line up)", () => {
    expect(highlightParts("İstanbul", "stan")).toEqual([{ text: "İstanbul", match: false }]);
  });
});
```

```
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { moveActive } from "./moveActive.ts";

describe("moveActive", () => {
  it("↓ with nothing active starts at the first row, ↑ at the last", () => {
    expect(moveActive(-1, 4, "down")).toBe(0);
    expect(moveActive(-1, 4, "up")).toBe(3);
  });

  it("moves one row", () => {
    expect(moveActive(1, 4, "down")).toBe(2);
    expect(moveActive(2, 4, "up")).toBe(1);
  });

  it("wraps around at both ends", () => {
    expect(moveActive(3, 4, "down")).toBe(0);
    expect(moveActive(0, 4, "up")).toBe(3);
  });

  it("an empty list has no active row", () => {
    expect(moveActive(-1, 0, "down")).toBe(-1);
    expect(moveActive(2, 0, "up")).toBe(-1);
  });

  it("an index past a (shorter) new list starts again from the first row", () => {
    expect(moveActive(5, 3, "down")).toBe(0);
  });
});
```

```
---
// @vitest-environment jsdom
import { act } from "react";
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { render } from "../test-utils.tsx";
import { useDebouncedValue } from "./useDebouncedValue.ts";

let shown: string[] = [];
function Probe({ value }: { value: string }) {
  shown.push(useDebouncedValue(value, 200));
  return null;
}

let cleanup: (() => void) | undefined;
beforeEach(() => {
  vi.useFakeTimers();
  shown = [];
});
afterEach(() => {
  cleanup?.();
  vi.useRealTimers();
});

describe("useDebouncedValue", () => {
  it("starts with the first value", () => {
    const view = render(<Probe value="a" />);
    cleanup = view.unmount;
    expect(shown.at(-1)).toBe("a");
  });

  it("gives a new value only after it stopped changing for the delay", () => {
    const view = render(<Probe value="w" />);
    cleanup = view.unmount;
    view.rerender(<Probe value="wi" />);
    act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(150));
    view.rerender(<Probe value="wit" />); // restarts the timer
    act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(150));
    expect(shown.at(-1)).toBe("w"); // 300 ms in total, but never 200 ms without a change
    act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(50));
    expect(shown.at(-1)).toBe("wit");
    expect(shown).not.toContain("wi"); // the in-between value was skipped
  });
});
```

## Claude · 2026-11-22T17:50:22.676Z

Timing in the test only: the answer lands one timer-tick after the debounce. I'll add a small `settle()` (a few short advances) after the debounce in the test helpers.

**Tool: Bash**

```
$ cd /c/PROJEKTAI/ENEBA/ENEBA-homework-13b && rm src/features/search/zz_probe.test.tsx && cat > "C:/Users/[USER]/AppData/Local/Temp/claude/27133936c4ab5668/5fdc6dc0-8eb6-4f11-88be-502305869da6/scratchpad/edit2.py" <<'EOF'
import sys
p = sys.argv[1] + "/src/features/search/SearchBox.test.tsx"
s = open(p, encoding="utf-8").read()
pairs = [
("""/** Lets time pass (the debounce, TanStack Query's timers) and the fake API answer. */
const wait = (ms: number) => act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(ms));""",
"""/** Lets time pass (the debounce, TanStack Query's timers) and the fake API answer. */
const wait = (ms: number) => act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(ms));
/**
 * Lets a started request finish and React show it. The answer is handed to React through a timer that only exists
 * AFTER the fake API answered, so one advance is not enough: take a few short steps.
```

```
// Command local runs the SAME router as the Vercel function on :8080,
// so the Go API can be developed without the Vercel CLI.
package main

import (
	"log"
	"net/http"

	"github.com/joho/godotenv"

	"github.com/dovydas472/ENEBA-homework/pkg/catalog"
	"github.com/dovydas472/ENEBA-homework/pkg/db"
	"github.com/dovydas472/ENEBA-homework/pkg/server"
)

func main() {
	// Load .env from the repo root (run this from the repo root).
	// godotenv is used ONLY here: on Vercel the variables come from project
	// settings. Variables already set in the environment win over .env.
	// A missing file is fine - the API still starts, /api/health will report 503.
	if err := godotenv.Load(".env"); err != nil {
		log.Printf("no .env loaded (%v) - using the process environment only", err)
	}

	lazyDB := db.NewLazy() // one pool, shared by health and the catalog
	router := server.NewRouter(server.Deps{DB: lazyDB, Catalog: catalog.NewStore(lazyDB)})

	addr := ":8080"
	log.Printf("local API listening on http://localhost%s", addr)
	log.Fatal(http.ListenAndServe(addr, router))
}
.env
```

```
// Task 13b: a SMALL local mock of the 13a search API, only for the first browser check before 13a is merged.
// Everything goes to the real local API (:8080, read-only GETs) except:
//  - GET /api/search/suggest?q=…  -> computed here from the real catalog (title substring + a tiny alias table)
//  - GET /api/products?q=…        -> the real listing without q, then filtered here by the same match (facets are NOT
//                                    recomputed: good enough to look at the page, the real check happens after the rebase)
import http from "node:http";

const UPSTREAM = "http://localhost:8080";
const PORT = Number(process.argv[2] ?? 8091);
const ALIASES = { "Grand Theft Auto V": ["GTA V", "GTA 5"], "Red Dead Redemption 2": ["RDR2"] };

async function allProducts() {
  const out = [];
  for (let page = 1; page < 10; page++) {
    const res = await fetch(`${UPSTREAM}/api/products?pageSize=48&page=${page}`);
    const body = await res.json();
    out.push(...body.items);
    if (out.length >= body.total) break;
  }
  return out; // popularity order
}

function match(title, q) {
  const t = title.toLowerCase();
  if (t.includes(q)) return { alias: null };
  const alias = (ALIASES[title] ?? []).find((a) => a.toLowerCase().includes(q));
  return alias ? { alias } : null;
}

function send(res, status, body) {
  res.writeHead(status, { "Content-Type": "application/json" });
  res.end(JSON.stringify(body));
}

http
  .createServer(async (req, res) => {
    const url = new URL(req.url, "http://x");
    const q = url.searchParams.get("q")?.trim().toLowerCase();
    try {
      if (url.pathname === "/api/search/suggest") {
        await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 250)); // a little latency, to see the spinner
        const games = new Map();
        for (const p of await allProducts()) {
          const m = match(p.title, q);
          if (!m) continue;
          const prev = games.get(p.title);
          const better = !prev || (p.inStock && (!prev.inStock || p.priceCents < prev.priceCents));
          if (better)
            games.set(p.title, {
              gameTitle: p.title,
              productSlug: p.slug,
              platform: p.platform,
```

```
// Task 13b: our search UI in headless Edge. Screenshots + axe (WCAG 2 A/AA, 2.1 AA, best practice) with the list open.
// Usage: node ours13.mjs <baseUrl> <desktop|mobile> <outPrefix> <query> [<query> ...]
// A query "store:<q>" opens /store?q=<q> instead of typing; "store-empty:<q>" too (the empty state).
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { createRequire } from "node:module";
import { launch, viewports, SCRATCH } from "./lib.mjs";

const require = createRequire(import.meta.url);
const axeSource = readFileSync(require.resolve("axe-core/axe.min.js"), "utf8");
const [base, kind, prefix, ...queries] = process.argv.slice(2);
const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
const browser = await launch();

async function newPage(path) {
  const page = await browser.newPage();
  await page.setViewport(viewports[kind]);
  await page.goto(base + path, { waitUntil: "networkidle0" });
  await sleep(500);
  return page;
}

async function axe(page, label) {
  await page.evaluate(axeSource);
  const result = await page.evaluate(() =>
    window.axe.run(document, { runOnly: { type: "tag", values: ["wcag2a", "wcag2aa", "wcag21aa", "best-practice"] } }),
  );
  const v = result.violations;
  console.log(`axe ${label}: ${v.length} violations`);
  for (const x of v) console.log(`  - ${x.id} (${x.impact}): ${x.help} -> ${x.nodes.map((n) => n.target.join(" ")).slice(0, 3).join(" | ")}`);
}

/** Facts about the combobox state, read from the DOM. */
async function state(page) {
  return page.evaluate(() => {
    const i = document.querySelector("input[role=combobox]");
    const lb = document.getElementById(i.getAttribute("aria-controls"));
    const pop = lb.parentElement;
    const r = pop.getBoundingClientRect();
    const opts = [...lb.querySelectorAll("[role=option]")].map((o) => o.textContent);
    return {
      expanded: i.getAttribute("aria-expanded"),
      active: i.getAttribute("aria-activedescendant"),
      popup: pop.hidden ? "hidden" : `x${Math.round(r.x)} y${Math.round(r.y)} w${Math.round(r.width)} h${Math.round(r.height)}`,
      options: opts,
      status: document.querySelector("[role=status]")?.textContent,
      overflowX: document.documentElement.scrollWidth > window.innerWidth,
    };
  });
}

for (const q of queries) {
  if (q.startsWith("store")) {
    const text = q.split(":")[1];
    const page = aw
```

Computing the new contrast pairs for DESIGN.md 5.1:

**Tool: Bash**

```
$ node -e '
const hex=h=>[1,3,5].map(i=>parseInt(h.slice(i,i+2),16));
const lin=c=>{c/=255;return c<=0.03928?c/12.92:((c+0.055)/1.055)**2.4};
const L=([r,g,b])=>0.2126*lin(r)+0.7152*lin(g)+0.0722*lin(b);
const mix=(fg,a,bg)=>fg.map((c,i)=>c*a+bg[i]*(1-a));
const ratio=(a,b)=>{const x=L(a),y=L(b);return ((Math.max(x,y)+.05)/(Math.min(x,y)+.05)).toFixed(2)};
const bg=hex("#4618ac"),deep=hex("#351183"),white=[255,255,255];
const muted=b=>mix(white,.7,b);
for (const [n,b] of [["bg",bg],["deep",deep]]) {
 console.log(n,"accent",ratio(hex("#fad318"),b),"muted",ratio(muted(b),b),"mint",ratio(hex("#23c299"),b),"nav",ratio(hex("#dcd8e6"),b),"subtle",ratio(hex("#b3aac9"),b),"white",ratio(white,b));
}'
```

```
# Task 12 – produkto puslapis ir krepšelio būsena

## Krepšelio taisyklės kaip „grynos" funkcijos (`src/features/cart/cart.ts`)
- **Ką daro:** `addItem`, `setQuantity`, `removeLine`, `cartCount`, `cartTotalCents`, `parseCart` – gauna dabartines eilutes ir grąžina **naujas**, niekada nekeičia gautų ir neliečia nei `localStorage`, nei React. Viena eilutė = vienas **pasiūlymas** (`offerId`): tas pats žaidimas iš kito pardavėjo – kita eilutė (C1).
- **Kiekio riba (C2):** `maxQuantity(stock) = min(stock, 5)`. Pridėjus tą patį pasiūlymą dar kartą kiekis +1, kol pasiekia ribą; tada rezultatas `"max"` ir rodomas „Maximum quantity reached". Jei pardavėjo atsargos sumažėjo, kiekis nuleidžiamas iki naujos ribos.
- **Kodėl taip:** grynas funkcijas lengva testuoti (29 testai be naršyklės) ir lengva paaiškinti; saugykla (`cartStore.ts`) tik laiko dabartinę reikšmę ir ją įrašo.
- **Gali paklausti:** „Kodėl suma skaičiuojama centais?" – pinigai visada sveiki centai, jokių `float` (CLAUDE.md). „Kas, jei kas nors į `localStorage` įrašo šiukšles?" – `parseCart` tikrina kiekvieną lauką (sveiki skaičiai, kiekis 1…riba, ne dublikatai); bet kas negerai → **tuščias krepšelis**, niekada ne klaida (C3). Papildomi laukai išmetami.

## Kodėl `localStorage` ir kodėl kaina ten – tik rodymui
- **Ką daro:** krepšelis saugomas naršyklėje po raktu `cart:v1`: `offerId`, `quantity` ir rodymo duomenys, užfiksuoti pridedant (pavadinimas, pardavėjas, kaina, atsargos…).
- **Kodėl `localStorage`:** prisijungimo dar 
… [truncated; original 6110 chars]
```

```
$ cd /c/PROJEKTAI/ENEBA/ENEBA-homework-13b && tail -c 200 docs/PAAISKINIMAI_LT.md | od -c | tail -3 && cat >> docs/PAAISKINIMAI_LT.md <<'EOF'

# Task 13b – paieškos UI: pasiūlymai header'yje ir paieškos rezultatai Store puslapyje

## Combobox + listbox (ARIA 1.2) – kaip veikia ir kodėl (`src/features/search/SearchBox.tsx`)
- **Ką daro:** paieškos laukas header'yje turi `role="combobox"`, o sąrašas po juo – `role="listbox"` su eilutėmis `role="option"`. Laukas su sąrašu susietas: `aria-controls` = sąrašo `id`, `aria-expanded` = ar sąrašas rodomas, `aria-autocomplete="list"`. Rodyklėmis ↓/↑ pasirenkama eilutė („aktyvi"), bet **fokusas lieka lauke**: laukas per `aria-activedescendant` pasako ekrano skaitytuvui, kurios eilutės `id` dabar aktyvi, o pati eilutė gauna `aria-selected="true"`.
- **Kodėl fokusas lieka lauke:** vartotojas gali toliau rašyti; jei fokusas šokinėtų į eilutes, kiekviena raidė ten nueitų niekur. Todėl eilutės ne Tab eilėje (`tabIndex={-1}`), o pelės paspaudimas ant eilutės daro `preventDefault` ant `mousedown` – fokusas neišeina iš lauko.
- **Klaviatūra (owner sprendimas Q2):** ↓/↑ – kita / ankstesnė eilutė, ratu (nuo paskutinės į pirmą; logika – gryna funkcija `moveActive.ts` su testais). Enter ant žaidimo eilutės → produkto puslapis; Enter be aktyvios eilutės arba ant „See all results" → `/store?q=…`. Esc – uždaro sąrašą, antras Esc – išvalo lauką (abiem atvejais `preventDefault`, kad naršyklės „Esc išvalo search lauką" nesimaišytų, ir `stopPropagation`, kad telefone paieškos eilutė neužsidarytų per anksti; trečias Esc telefone uždaro paieškos eilutę). Tab ir paspaudimas už sąrašo ribų – uždaro.
- **Eilutė – tikra nuoroda (`<a role="option">`):** veikia vidurinis pelės mygtukas ir „atidaryti naujame skirtuke". HTML leidžia `<a href>` turėti `role="option"`.
- **„No matches":** kai atsakymas tuščias, rodoma viena eilutė „No matches for 'q'", bet `aria-expanded="false"` – nes listbox'e nėra nė vieno pasirinkimo. Ekrano skaitytuvui apie rezultatą praneša atskiras, **visada DOM'e esantis** `role="status"` regionas („6 games found…" / „No matches for 'q'") – ta pati priežastis kaip toast'o (Task 12): skaitytuvas praneša tik apie pokyčius jau esamame regione.
- **Gali paklausti:** „Kodėl ne `<datalist>` ar `<select>`?" – `<datalist>` neleidžia viršelių, kainų, nuorodų ir stiliaus; ARIA combobox yra standartinis W3C APG modelis tokiam sąrašui. „Kodėl listbox visada DOM'e?" – `aria-controls` turi rodyti į egzistuojantį elementą; uždarytas jis t
… [truncated; original 8862 chars]
```

```
# Task 13b – paieškos UI: pasiūlymai header'yje ir paieškos rezultatai Store puslapyje

## Combobox + listbox (ARIA 1.2) – kaip veikia ir kodėl (`src/features/search/SearchBox.tsx`)
- **Ką daro:** paieškos laukas header'yje turi `role="combobox"`, o sąrašas po juo – `role="listbox"` su eilutėmis `role="option"`. Laukas su sąrašu susietas: `aria-controls` = sąrašo `id`, `aria-expanded` = ar sąrašas rodomas, `aria-autocomplete="list"`. Rodyklėmis ↓/↑ pasirenkama eilutė („aktyvi"), bet **fokusas lieka lauke**: laukas per `aria-activedescendant` pasako ekrano skaitytuvui, kurios eilutės `id` dabar aktyvi, o pati eilutė gauna `aria-selected="true"`.
- **Kodėl fokusas lieka lauke:** vartotojas gali toliau rašyti; jei fokusas šokinėtų į eilutes, kiekviena raidė ten nueitų niekur. Todėl eilutės ne Tab eilėje (`tabIndex={-1}`), o pelės paspaudimas ant eilutės daro `preventDefault` ant `mousedown` – fokusas neišeina iš lauko.
- **Klaviatūra (owner sprendimas Q2):** ↓/↑ – kita / ankstesnė eilutė, ratu (nuo paskutinės į pirmą; logika – gryna funkcija `moveActive.ts` su testais). Enter ant žaidimo eilutės → produkto puslapis; Enter be aktyvios eilutės arba ant „See all results" → `/store?q=…`. Esc – uždaro sąrašą, antras Esc – išvalo lauką (abiem atvejais `preventDefault`, kad nesimaišytų naršyklės „Esc išvalo search lauką", ir `stopPropagation`, kad telefone paieškos eilutė neužsidarytų per anksti; trečias Esc telefone uždaro paieškos eilutę). Tab ir paspaudimas už sąrašo ribų – uždaro.
- **Eilutė – tikra nuoroda (`<a role="option">`):** veikia vidurinis pelės mygtukas ir „atidaryti naujame skirtuke". HTML leidžia `<a href>` turėti `role="option"`.
- **„No matches":** kai atsakymas tuščias, rodoma viena eilutė „No matches for 'q'", bet `aria-expanded="false"` – nes listbox'e nėra nė vieno pasirinkimo. Ekrano skaitytuvui apie rezultatą praneša atskiras, **visada DOM'e esantis** `role="status"` regionas („6 games found…" / „No matches for 'q'") – ta pati priežastis kaip toast'o (Task 12): skaitytuvas praneša tik apie pokyčius jau esamame regione.
- **Gali paklausti:** „Kodėl ne `<datalist>`?" – `<datalist>` neleidžia viršelių, kainų, nuorodų ir stiliaus; ARIA combobox yra standartinis W3C APG modelis tokiam sąrašui. „Kodėl listbox visada DOM'e?" – `aria-controls` turi rodyti į egzistuojantį elementą; uždarytas jis tiesiog `hidden`.
```

Task 13b is built and tested on `task/13b-search-ui`, but it isn't pushed and has no PR yet. The task says to rebase on `main` after 13a is merged, and 13a's PR #13 is still open.

**What's on the branch** (10 commits, in the worktree `../ENEBA-homework-13b`):
- **Header search suggestions:** an accessible dropdown (ARIA combobox + listbox) on desktop and phone. Up to 6 games and a "See all results for 'q'" row, all your Q2 rules (2+ chars, 200 ms debounce, keyboard, mouse, spinner, "No matches") and prefetch on hover. Laid out like Eneba's popup, which I captured at 1440 and 390 px; the screenshots are outside the repo.
- **Store with `q`:** `q` is sent to the API, with the "Results for 'q'" heading and "Clear search". "Best match" appears only when `q` is set, and the URL is cleaned up when it isn't. Empty searches show "No results for 'q'" plus a "Popular right now" grid of 8.

- **Browser check:** I ran 13a's unmerged backend code locally on port 8092 against the `dev` branch. axe found 0 violations with the dropdown open and with a row selected, on desktop and phone, and there is no sideways scrolling. Ten screenshots are committed.
- **Bug found and fixed in the browser:** on phones the "Close search" × was dimmed by the backdrop. Now only the page under the header row dims, as on Eneba.
- **Review:** the task-reviewer said "changes needed" for two small items: the response type name (now `SuggestResponse`, same as 13a) and an outdated `SearchField` mention in the Lithuanian doc. Both are fixed.

```
# Task 13b – paieškos UI: pasiūlymai header'yje ir paieškos rezultatai Store puslapyje

## Combobox + listbox (ARIA 1.2) – kaip veikia ir kodėl (`src/features/search/SearchBox.tsx`)
- **Ką daro:** paieškos laukas header'yje turi `role="combobox"`, o sąrašas po juo – `role="listbox"` su eilutėmis `role="option"`. Laukas su sąrašu susietas: `aria-controls` = sąrašo `id`, `aria-expanded` = ar sąrašas rodomas, `aria-autocomplete="list"`. Rodyklėmis ↓/↑ pasirenkama eilutė („aktyvi"), bet **fokusas lieka lauke**: laukas per `aria-activedescendant` pasako ekrano skaitytuvui, kurios eilutės `id` dabar aktyvi, o pati eilutė gauna `aria-selected="true"`.
```

**Tool: Write**

```
c:\PROJEKTAI\ENEBA\ENEBA-homework-13b\cmd\local\main.go
---
// Command local runs the SAME router as the Vercel function on 127.0.0.1:8080,
// so the Go API can be developed without the Vercel CLI.
//
//	go run ./cmd/local             # http://127.0.0.1:8080
//	go run ./cmd/local -port 8092  # another port, e.g. next to an older server
package main

import (
	"flag"
	"fmt"
	"log"
	"net"
	"net/http"
	"strconv"

	"github.com/joho/godotenv"

	"github.com/dovydas472/ENEBA-homework/pkg/catalog"
	"github.com/dovydas472/ENEBA-homework/pkg/db"
	"github.com/dovydas472/ENEBA-homework/pkg/server"
)

// listenAddr is where the local API listens: always the loopback address 127.0.0.1, never ":port"
// (= every network interface). Only this computer can reach it, and Windows Firewall does not ask
// whether to open the port to the network.
func listenAddr(port int) (string, error) {
	if port < 1 || port > 65535 {
		return "", fmt.Errorf("port must be 1-65535, got %d", port)
	}
	return net.JoinHostPort("127.0.0.1", strconv.Itoa(port)), nil
}

func main() {
	port := flag.Int("port", 8080, "TCP port on 127.0.0.1")
	flag.Parse()
	addr, err := listenAddr(*port)
	if err != nil {
		log.Fatal(err)
	}

	// Load .env from the repo root (run this from the repo root).
	// godotenv is used ONLY here: on Vercel the variables come from project
	// settings. Variables already set in the environment win over .env.
	// A missing file is fine - the API still starts, /api/health will report 503.
	if err := godotenv.Load(".env"); err != nil {
		log.Printf("no .env loaded (%v) - using the process environment only", err)
	}

	lazyDB := db.NewLazy() // one pool, shared by health and the catalog
	router := server.NewRouter(server.Deps{DB: lazyDB, Catalog: catalog.NewStore(lazyDB)})

	log.Printf("local API listening on http://%s", addr)
	log.Fatal(http.ListenAndServe(addr, router))
}
```

**Tool: Write**

```
---
package main

import "testing"

func TestListenAddr_loopbackOnly(t *testing.T) {
	for _, port := range []int{8080, 8092, 1, 65535} {
		addr, err := listenAddr(port)
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatalf("port %d: unexpected error %v", port, err)
		}
		want := "127.0.0.1:" + itoa(port)
		if addr != want {
			t.Errorf("port %d: got %q, want %q", port, addr, want)
		}
	}
}

func TestListenAddr_rejectsInvalidPorts(t *testing.T) {
	for _, port := range []int{0, -1, 65536} {
		if _, err := listenAddr(port); err == nil {
			t.Errorf("port %d: expected an error", port)
		}
	}
}

func itoa(n int) string {
	if n == 0 {
		return "0"
	}
	s := ""
	for ; n > 0; n /= 10 {
		s = string(rune('0'+n%10)) + s
	}
	return s
}
```

```
package main

import "testing"

func TestListenAddr_loopbackOnly(t *testing.T) {
	cases := map[int]string{
		8080:  "127.0.0.1:8080",
		8092:  "127.0.0.1:8092",
		1:     "127.0.0.1:1",
		65535: "127.0.0.1:65535",
	}
	for port, want := range cases {
		got, err := listenAddr(port)
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatalf("port %d: unexpected error %v", port, err)
		}
		if got != want {
			t.Errorf("port %d: got %q, want %q", port, got, want)
		}
	}
}

func TestListenAddr_rejectsInvalidPorts(t *testing.T) {
	for _, port := range []int{0, -1, 65536} {
		if _, err := listenAddr(port); err == nil {
			t.Errorf("port %d: expected an error", port)
		}
	}
}
```

```
import { defineConfig } from "vite";
import react from "@vitejs/plugin-react";

// Dev proxy: the browser calls /api/... on the Vite port, Vite forwards it to
// the local Go server (cmd/local) so there are no CORS issues in development.
// Everything local listens on 127.0.0.1 only (CLAUDE.md). The proxy target is 127.0.0.1, not "localhost":
// Node may resolve "localhost" to the IPv6 ::1, where cmd/local does not listen.
export default defineConfig({
  plugins: [react()],
  server: {
    host: "127.0.0.1",
    proxy: {
      "/api": "http://127.0.0.1:8080",
    },
  },
  preview: {
    host: "127.0.0.1",
  },
});
```

```
import sys

root = sys.argv[1]


def edit(path, pairs):
    full = f"{root}/{path}"
    s = open(full, encoding="utf-8").read()
    for a, b in pairs:
        assert a in s, (path, a[:70])
        s = s.replace(a, b)
    open(full, "w", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n").write(s)


edit(
    "README.md",
    [
        (
            """# terminal 1 – Go API on :8080 (run from the repo root so .env is found)
go run ./cmd/local

# terminal 2 – Vite dev server on :5173 (proxies /api -> :8080)
npm run dev
```


**Tool: Write**

```
C:\Users\[USER]\.claude\projects\c--PROJEKTAI-ENEBA-ENEBA-homework\memory\local-servers-loopback.md
---
---
name: local-servers-loopback
description: Local servers and temporary check programs bind 127.0.0.1 only and live in the scratchpad, never in the repo
metadata:
  type: feedback
---

Every local server started for this project (cmd/local, Vite dev/preview, mock APIs, one-off check servers) must listen on 127.0.0.1 only, never ":port" / 0.0.0.0. Temporary check programs live in the session scratchpad, never under cmd/ or uncommitted inside a repo worktree.

**Why:** listening on all interfaces makes Windows Firewall prompt the owner every time (it happened with a throwaway `cmd/zz13bcheck` server in Task 13b). The rule is in CLAUDE.md since Task 13b.

**How to apply:** use `go run ./cmd/local -port N` (binds 127.0.0.1) instead of writing a server; node mocks use `server.listen(port, "127.0.0.1")`; Vite gets `--host 127.0.0.1`; use `127.0.0.1` (not `localhost`) in proxy targets and curl, since Node may resolve localhost to ::1. Note that cmd/local reads `.env` from the cwd, so in a worktree without `.env` run it from the main folder's checkout or pass the env another way.
```

The first rows are right (`gt` gives the four GTA games), but the rest of the list fills up with matches in the middle of words (`re` brings up Dishonored and San Andreas) and inside foreign-language aliases (`di` brings up "Zaklínač 3: Divoký hon"). I recommend a small backend follow-up so 2-letter searches only match word starts. Report §2 has the table, §6 the options.
- **127.0.0.1 only:** `cmd/local` now listens on `127.0.0.1` with a `-port` flag (default 8080). Vite dev and preview bind `127.0.0.1` too, and its `/api` proxy target is `127.0.0.1`, not `localhost`. I checked with `netstat` that nothing listens on `0.0.0.0`. The rule is in CLAUDE.md and saved to memory.
- **Temporary programs:** `cmd/zz13bcheck` was never committed. It was deleted with the throwaway worktree, and the 13b worktree is removed too.
- **Your old servers:** the ones from 2026-11-21 (ports 8080, 5173, 4173) still run with the old code and still listen on all interfaces. Restart them from `main` after merging.
